intdb {
	bel_class SLICE {
		input A[2];
		input B[2];
		input C[2];
		input D[2];
		input M[2];
		input FXA;
		input FXB;
		input CLK;
		input LSR;
		input CE;
		input WCK;
		input WRE;
		output F[2];
		output Q[2];
	}

	bel_class EBR {
		input CLKA;
		input CLKB;
		input RSTA;
		input RSTB;
		input CEA;
		input CEB;
		input WEA;
		input WEB;
		input OCEA;
		input OCEB;
		input CSA[3];
		input CSB[3];
		input ADA[13];
		input ADB[13];
		input DIA[9];
		input DIB[9];
		output DOA[9];
		output DOB[9];
	}

	bel_class IO {
		input CLK;
		input LSR;
		input CE;
		input ECLK;
		input SLIP;
		input TXDATA[16];
		input TSDATA[1];
		input DIRECTION;
		input LOADN;
		input MOVE;
		input HSSEL;
		output PADDI;
		output DI;
		output RXDATA[16];
		output CFLAG;
		output INFF;
		pad PAD: inout
	}

	bel_class DDRDLL {
		input CLK;
		input RST;
		input UDDCNTLN;
		input FREEZE;
		output LOCK;
		output DCNTL[8];
		output DIVOSC;
		output DDRDEL;
	}

	bel_class DLLDEL {
		input A;
		input DDRDEL;
		input DIRECTION;
		input LOADN;
		input MOVE;
		output Z;
		output CFLAG;
	}

	bel_class BCINRD {
		input INRDENI;
	}

	bel_class BCLVDSO {
		input LVDSENI;
	}

	bel_class DCC {
		input CLKI;
		input CE;
		output CLKO;
	}

	bel_class DCS {
		input CLK[2];
		input SEL;
		output DCSOUT;
	}

	bel_class DCM {
		input CLK[2];
		input SEL;
		output DCMOUT;
	}

	bel_class CLKTEST {
		input TESTIN[24];
	}

	bel_class CLKDIV {
		input CLKI;
		input RST;
		input ALIGNWD;
		output CDIVX;
	}

	bel_class GSR {
		input CLK;
		input GSR;
	}

	bel_class OSC {
		input HFOUTEN;
		input HFCLKDIV[2];
		output LFCLKOUT;
		output HFCLKOUT;
	}

	bel_class PMU {
		input SLEEP;

				mux IMUX_LSR[0] = X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X2_W1[3] | X2_W1[5] | X2_W2[3] | X2_W2[5] | X2_E1[3] | X2_E1[5] | X2_E2[3] | X2_E2[5] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_LSR[1] = X0_S[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X0_N[1] | X2_W1[3] | X2_W1[5] | X2_W2[3] | X2_W2[5] | X2_E1[3] | X2_E1[5] | X2_E2[3] | X2_E2[5] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[0] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[2] | X2_S2[2] | X2_N1[2] | X2_N2[2] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[1] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[2] | X2_S2[2] | X2_N1[2] | X2_N2[2] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[2] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[6] | X2_S2[6] | X2_N1[6] | X2_N2[6] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[3] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X2_W1[1] | X2_W2[1] | X2_E1[1] | X2_E2[1] | X2_S1[6] | X2_S2[6] | X2_N1[6] | X2_N2[6] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
			}
		}

			cell CELL[1];

			bel PLL[0] {
				input CLKFB = CELL[1].IMUX_CLK[0];

				input PHASESEL1 = CELL[1].IMUX_B[4];

		tile_class BC {
			cell CELL;

			switchbox BEL_INT {
				mux IMUX_DDRDLL_CLK = IMUX_CLK[0] | ECLK[0] | ECLK[1];
			}

			bel DDRDLL {
				input CLK = IMUX_DDRDLL_CLK;
				input RST = IMUX_LSR[0];
				input UDDCNTLN = IMUX_B[0];
				input FREEZE = IMUX_A[0];
				output LOCK = OUT_Q[0];
				output DCNTL[0] = OUT_F[0];
				output DCNTL[1] = OUT_F[1];
				output DCNTL[2] = OUT_F[2];
				output DCNTL[3] = OUT_F[3];
				output DCNTL[4] = OUT_F[4];
				output DCNTL[5] = OUT_F[5];
				output DCNTL[6] = OUT_F[6];
				output DCNTL[7] = OUT_F[7];
				output DIVOSC = OUT_Q[1];
				output DDRDEL = OUT_DDRDLL_DDRDEL;
			}

			bel BCINRD {
				input INRDENI = IMUX_B[4];
			}

			bel BCLVDSO {
				input LVDSENI = IMUX_A[4];
			}

			// wire IMUX_A[0]                      DDRDLL.FREEZE
			// wire IMUX_A[4]                      BCLVDSO.LVDSENI
			// wire IMUX_B[0]                      DDRDLL.UDDCNTLN
